# Andor S01E10 Selected-Envelope Picture Evidence — V0.1

## A. Source and applicability audit

- Evidence ID: `DM-ANDOR-S01E10-SEL-001`
- Work / season / episode: source/filename-supplied as *Andor* / Season 1 / Episode 10; not independently established from picture
- Local source filename (media stays local): `安多S01E10.mp4`
- Uploaded/local duration: `00:42:47.750`
- Video basis: 1920×1080 H.264 picture, exact 24 fps; global file-frame indexing
- Continuous scene: `PARTIAL` — one contiguous selected edit envelope; start is internal and is not claimed to be the full natural scene start
- Original dialogue and ambience preserved: source has one audio track, but it was not auditioned; `UNKNOWN` for every sound claim
- Analyzed source time range: `[F34855,F44568)` = `00:24:12.292–00:30:57.000` end-exclusive; last retained frame `F44567 / 00:30:56.958`
- Locator disposition: the backlog locator was used only to navigate. The selected envelope was fixed from picture evidence.
- Why this scene tests the selected problem: OBSERVED multi-zone control-room, room/corridor/stair, threshold and exterior coverage offers a single-source test of spatial re-registration and body-state tracking. Escape, command, rally, leadership, power transfer, broadcasting and reception are not picture facts here.
- Suitability: `PARTIAL` — suitable for picture grammar and state-continuity candidates; unsuitable for sound/dialogue or semantic causality without separately locked script/audio evidence.

### Visual aliases

`P-A-like`, `P-B-like`, `P-C-like` and `P-D-like` are visual-track handles based on recurring visible hair/wardrobe/object features. Cross-cut grouping is `INFERRED`; exact identity, role, name and age remain `UNKNOWN`. Compact dark long objects and compact foreground objects are described by visible form only; type, ownership and use remain `UNKNOWN`.

## B. Scene dramatic structure

- Start state — `OBSERVED`: the selected envelope begins on a hard cut into a bright control-room registration with grey-uniformed and white-clad people, consoles and display geometry (`ANDOR-W4-S001`). It is an internal selected start, not a verified natural scene beginning.
- Character objectives — `UNKNOWN`: no objective, escape plan, order, rally or leadership state is established from picture alone.
- Obstacle — `UNKNOWN`: costumes, raised hands, compact objects, doorways and crowds cannot independently prove custody, threat, control or resistance.
- Event point — `INFERRED` only: the visible room darkening and cross-zone inserts (`S025–S030`) form a picture-level state update. Cause and dramatic meaning are `UNKNOWN`.
- Reaction point — `UNKNOWN`: face/body changes occur, but no stimulus or reaction causality is proven without sound/script.
- Turn — `INFERRED` only: group movement becomes dominant around corridor/stair coverage (`S076–S100`) and later reaches a bright exterior threshold (`S104–S119`). Escape/rally meaning is locked-script-gated.
- End state — `OBSERVED`: two top-down views show industrial facility geometry, dark water and numerous distant pale person-like forms (`S120–S121`). Their identities, locomotion and outcomes are `UNKNOWN`.
- POV — `INFERRED`: objective editorial coverage; no optical character POV verified.
- Audience information — `OBSERVED`: changes in visible space, scale, body/object position and lighting. All dialogue, command, broadcast, message reception and sound remain `UNKNOWN`.

## D. Coverage and spatial design

- `P0 / S001–S030`: control-room near/far zones, console/display anchors, body/object relations, a visible light-state cycle and distinct exterior/recessed-room/corridor inserts.
- `P1 / S031–S051`: multi-person blocking update plus tighter alternation around grey-uniformed, dark-haired white-clad and older white-clad visual tracks.
- `P2 / S052–S075`: remote-looking room/recess/corridor/stair units alternate with a central older face/compact-object track. Broadcast/listener causality is `UNKNOWN`.
- `P3 / S076–S100`: corridor axes, doorway route, crowded stairs, high vertical views, control/display inserts and an exterior conduit increase geographic scale.
- `P4 / S101–S121`: narrow opening/dim room, crowd approach to a bright threshold, edge/body-state views and two distant facility/water resets.
- Across cuts, exact same-person, same-object and same-location identity is never promoted above `INFERRED`; off-frame continuity stays `UNKNOWN`.

## E. Editing and rhythm

- Shot count: 121
- Total selected duration: 404.708333 s / 9713 frames
- Mean duration: 3.344697 s
- Median duration: 2.583333 s
- Minimum: 0.291667 s (ANDOR-W4-S084)
- Maximum: 15.750000 s (ANDOR-W4-S001)
- Distribution: <1.0 s = 6; 1.0–<2.0 s = 33; 2.0–<4.0 s = 48; 4.0–<8.0 s = 29; >=8.0 s = 5
- These statistics describe this selected envelope only and do not establish a general pacing rule.

- All 120 internal boundaries plus selected in/out boundaries were manually accepted from visible picture.
- Rapid shots include `S084` (7 frames), `S116` (10 frames) and `S117` (17 frames); their semantic purpose remains `UNKNOWN`.
- Long holds do not prove dialogue ownership: for example, the older face/object interval in `S058` is picture-only.

## F. Sound and performance grammar

- `SND-UNK` for every shot: the audio track was not directly auditioned.
- Dialogue, commands, chanting/rallying, PA/broadcast, ambience, silence, overlap, music, impacts, breath, off-screen cues and sound-led cuts are `UNKNOWN`.
- Visible mouth, gaze, posture, hand, crowd and 2D body-overlap changes are `OBSERVED` only at the picture level. Physical contact existence/depth/force, speech, emotion, intention, coercion, leadership, aid and reaction cause are `UNKNOWN`.
- Burned subtitles were excluded from every picture and semantic claim.

## H. Transfer candidates

Each item is a single-source hypothesis, not a universal rule. Triggers are locked-script-gated and all generation fallbacks use project-original characters, spaces, objects and events.

| rule_id | trigger | directing decision | default coverage | blocking | pacing/edit | sound | applicability | non-applicability | failure mode | counterexample | UNKNOWN | AI risk | fallback | evidence shots | confidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| ANDOR-W4-C01 | LOCKED-SCRIPT-GATED: a project-original multi-person control space requires legible opposing zones, a visible body-state rearrangement and one light/space update; command, custody and authority meanings must already exist in the locked script | HYPOTHESIS: establish the room in shared reverses, isolate only the bodies/objects whose visible state changes, then use one project-original light or spatial reset; never copy this source facility, wardrobe, props, displays or event | project-original wide registration → two or three state-relevant singles/relations → shared blocking update → one light/space reset | keep console/door equivalents on stable project-original sides; show hands/objects only when required; never infer rank from costume or frame dominance | source observation only: S001–S024 alternate 24 shots before S025–S030 light/space updates; durations are descriptive, not defaults | SND-UNK: audio not auditioned; commands, alarms, impacts, silence, dialogue and sound-led cuts UNKNOWN | original scenes whose locked script requires both geography and a visible multi-body/object/light state transition | single-person work; no authored state rearrangement; geography already established; or a continuous master preserves every required state | coverage can over-fragment a simple state change, drift hands/objects, or visually imply hierarchy/coercion absent from the script | SOURCE-OBSERVED / different-trigger context exclusion: S058 holds one older face/object view for 11.875s instead of re-registering the room; this does not establish a same-trigger contrary case, which remains pending | roles, exact identities, command, coercion, authority transfer, object function, dialogue, sound, simultaneity and authorial cut intent | camera=HIGH for multi-axis shared/reverse/light reset; semantics and transfer trigger remain locked-script-gated |

## I. Boundary

### VERIFIED FACT / OBSERVED

- Selected envelope: `[F34855,F44568)` / `00:24:12.292–00:30:57.000` end-exclusive; 9713 frames, 121 gap-free shots.
- `F34855` is a visible hard cut into a fresh control-room registration and is an internal selected start.
- `F44568` is a visible hard cut from the facility exterior to a distinct industrial/crowd thread and is excluded.
- Picture-only states, positions and changes are recorded with exact shot/frame/time references.

### EVIDENCE-BASED INFERENCE

- Phase labels, editorial POV, cut motivation, narrative function, visual-track grouping, cross-zone relation and chronology are interpretive and remain explicitly `INFERRED`.

### HYPOTHESIS / candidate rule

- `ANDOR-W4-C01–C04` are transferable decision-logic candidates from this one source only. They require counter-source testing before promotion.

### UNKNOWN

- Full natural scene start; audio/dialogue; command, rally, power transfer, escape objective; names, roles, exact identities/ages and relationships; object/device type; broadcast/message causality; emotion/intention; physical-contact existence/force; jump/fall choice, landing, swimming or survival; lens/support and production method.

### Never copy into original work

- Do not copy source characters, faces, costumes, facility architecture, props, displays, signature compositions, dialogue/subtitles, event sequence or copyrighted surface design. Transfer only abstract, project-original decision logic after locked-script approval.
